sakuya_building_add_bog_iron_smelter = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:bog_iron_smelter
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:bog_iron_smelter value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

sakuya_building_select_stone_quarry_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 16
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_stone_quarry = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:stone_quarry
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:stone_quarry value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:iron_foundry
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:iron_foundry value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:steel_mill value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_index value = 59 }

sakuya_building_select_firearms_manufactory_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 59
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_firearms_manufactory = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:firearms_manufactory
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:firearms_manufactory value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_brewery_manufactory = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:brewery_manufactory
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:brewery_manufactory value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:furniture_manufactory
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:furniture_manufactory value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:scriptorium value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_category value = 5 }
		set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_index value = 116 }
	}

sakuya_building_select_minting_office_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 116
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_minting_office = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:minting_office
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:minting_office value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:calmecac
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:calmecac value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:tur_kodjabashi_office
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:tur_kodjabashi_office value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_building_select_pirate_stronghold_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 173
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_pirate_stronghold = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:pirate_stronghold
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:pirate_stronghold value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:monastery
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:monastery value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:debere_meshwae value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
	}

	}

		if = {
			limit = {

				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}

			}

		else = {
			if = {

				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}

			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:university value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }

sakuya_building_add_venetian_palaces = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:venetian_palaces
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:venetian_palaces value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_shown = { always = yes }

	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:doges_palace
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:doges_palace value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}

				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:the_cipher_secretary value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_building_add_smuggler_cove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:smuggler_cove
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:smuggler_cove value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 300
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_funduq = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:funduq
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:funduq value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

	effect = { }

sakuya_building_add_ori_portuguese_quarters = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:ori_portuguese_quarters
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:ori_portuguese_quarters value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:armory value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_building_add_gallowglass_sept = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:gallowglass_sept
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:gallowglass_sept value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_valid = { always = yes }
	effect = {
		set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_category value = 11 }
		set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_index value = 357 }
	}
}

sakuya_building_select_safaviyya_order_hall_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 357
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_safaviyya_order_hall = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:safaviyya_order_hall
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:safaviyya_order_hall value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 371
	}

sakuya_building_add_bastion = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:bastion
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:bastion value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:korean_gunnery_land_defense
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:korean_gunnery_land_defense value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:castel_sant_angelo value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_building_add_market_village = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:market_village
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:market_village value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_building_select_festival_grounds_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_index
		var:sakuya_building_picker_index = 428
	}
	effect = { }
}

sakuya_building_add_festival_grounds = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:festival_grounds
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:festival_grounds value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}

		any_owned_location = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_index
				var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_index = 0
			}
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				destroy_all_buildings_of_type = building_type:nobled_sponsored_temple
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value } }
				set_variable = { name = sakuya_building_picker_amount_value value = 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = player_country
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
				change_building_level_in_location = { building = building_type:nobled_sponsored_temple value = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_building_picker_amount_value }
			}
		}
	}
}
